Use when the user wants to benchmark on RNA-Puzzles & FARFAR2 Decoys, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports RMSD. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-5hmc-site-classification))

- **rna-chemical-shift-prediction**: Use when assigned RNA chemical shifts and a 3D RNA structure (PDB coordinates) are available and per-atom-type predicted shifts for 1H, 13C, or 15N are needed, e.g. for assignment validation or NMR structure assessment. - **rna-count-matrix-aggregation**: Use when multiple small RNA-seq samples have been annotated by COMPSRA and per-sample tab-delimited RNA count files must be merged into one RNA-by-sample count matrix before downstream normalization or differential expression. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-count-matrix-aggregation))
- **rna-coverage-angle-based-on-off-classification**: Use when preparing base-level RNA-seq pileup matrices (e.g., TCGA cohort) for SCISSOR shape-change detection and genes with many non-expressed samples must be identified and excluded. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-coverage-angle-based-on-off-classification))
- **rna-design-strategy**: Route RNA secondary-structure inverse folding, multi-strand nucleic-acid system design, 3D RNA inverse design, mRNA coding-sequence optimization, and siRNA/ASO targeting through professional task-specific methods. Select verified local tools or equivalent MCP/API/remote compute from live readiness evidence. ([Victor-Xu-1/X-Science](https://github.com/Victor-Xu-1/X-Science/tree/HEAD/skills/x-science/rna-design-strategy) · ★41)
- **rna-dna-interaction-dataset-curation**: Use when building a gold-standard benchmark of experimentally validated lncRNA:DNA binding sites from heterogeneous published ChIRP/ChOP/CHART/RAP-seq studies, requiring uniform peak-calling and reproducibility classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/rna-dna-interaction-dataset-curation))
- **rna-dna-modification-site-annotation**: Use when you have an aligned nanopore modBAM (from Dorado basecalling + minimap2 mapping) with MM/ML base-modification tags and need per-site modification calls filtered to reliable positions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/rna-dna-modification-site-annotation))
- **rna-dna-triplex-prediction-benchmarking**: Use when comparing the prediction accuracy or runtime of triplex-forming lncRNA tools on experimentally validated RNA:DNA peaks and needing a fair, negative-control-based AUC benchmark with resampling-based confidence estimates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/rna-dna-triplex-prediction-benchmarking))
- **rna-editing-annotation-warning-logic**: Use when annotating plastid genomes for RNA editing and a translated CDS accumulates internal stops, to decide whether stops are editable nonsense mutations or signs of frameshifted assembly/annotation errors. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/rna-editing-annotation-warning-logic))
- **rna-editing-cluster-interval-extension**: Use when you have a BED file of high-confidence RNA-editing clusters from the MAPIT-seq hc_cluster step and need to convert them into sequence windows for de novo RBP-binding motif discovery (e.g. testing RBFOX2 UGCAUG enrichment). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/rna-editing-cluster-interval-extension))
- **rna-editing-event-detection-gatk**: Use when you have MAPIT-seq BAM alignments (post-mapping and finetuning) with hADAR2dd and rAPOBEC1 deamination and need to call adjacent A-to-G and C-to-U editing events to mark RBP-bound transcripts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/rna-editing-event-detection-gatk))
- **rna-editing-fraction-per-cluster-aggregation**: Use when a long-read single-cell dataset (UMI consensus BAM from ScNaUmi-seq/Sicelore) must be interrogated for RNA editing site usage across cell populations or maturation states, quantified as edited/total molecules per cluster group. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/rna-editing-fraction-per-cluster-aggregation))
- **rna-editing-pileup-generation**: Use when REDInet inference needs tabix-indexed REDItools outTables from coordinate-sorted RNAseq BAMs, or when benchmarking REDItools v1 vs v3 pileup runtime on very deep HEK293T libraries. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-editing-pileup-generation))
- **rna-editing-site-calling**: Use when MAPIT-seq alignments (Mapit_result directory) are ready and you must detect C-to-U and A-to-G editing events from hADAR2dd and rAPOBEC1 to pinpoint RBP-binding regions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/rna-editing-site-calling))
- **rna-editing-site-deep-learning-inference**: Use when you have tabix-indexed REDItools outTables of candidate A-to-G substitutions from RNAseq BAMs and need to distinguish genuine ADAR-mediated editing sites from SNPs or artifacts, especially when matched DNAseq data is unavailable. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-editing-site-deep-learning-inference))
- **rna-editing-site-density-windowing**: Use when constructing a positive training set of double-stranded RNA regions from A-to-I RNA editing evidence — i.e., when converting REDIportal editing-site annotations into structurally verified dsRNA coordinates for model training. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-editing-site-density-windowing))
- **rna-essentiality-screen-analysis**: Use when a ranked list of candidate cis driver genes (e.g., TCGA Rank-1 iEDGE genes) must be validated against DepMap RNAi essentiality screens where dependency scores correlate with copy number amplification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/rna-essentiality-screen-analysis))
- **rna-figure-workflow**: Create and audit common RNA-seq result figures locally, including volcano, PCA, heatmap, expression boxplot, and enrichment dotplot, with PNG/PDF delivery and inline conversation preview. Use for RNA result plotting from user-selected tables; do not use private bundled datasets. ([MiniMax-AI/MiniMax-Code-Plugins](https://github.com/MiniMax-AI/MiniMax-Code-Plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/Presisitence/bio-research-forge/skills/rna-figure-workflow) · ★25)
- **rna-kinetics-simulation**: Use when validating or benchmarking a kinetic-rate inference pipeline for RNA metabolic labelling data: you need synthetic ground-truth synthesis/processing/degradation rates to measure inference accuracy (Spearman correlations) or to tune experimental design (e.g. number of replicates). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/rna-kinetics-simulation))
- **rna-m5c-sequence-dataset-handling**: Use when the input is RNA cytosine-site sequence data (FASTA windows around candidate m5C sites) that must be split into species-specific positive/negative training and independent test sets for m5C prediction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-m5c-sequence-dataset-handling))
- **rna-m5c-sequence-feature-fusion**: Use when building sequence-based RNA modification (m5C) predictors that need richer features than a single encoding, and fused-feature dimensionality or training cost must be reduced with a filter method such as F score, MRMD, or PCC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-m5c-sequence-feature-fusion))
- **rna-m5c-site-prediction**: Use when query RNA sequences in FASTA format need m5C site prediction for A. thaliana or M. musculus, or when reproducing/benchmarking stacked-ensemble m5C predictors against SVM/RF baselines like m5CPred-SVM or iRNA-m5C. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-m5c-site-prediction))
- **rna-m6a-site-prediction**: Use when you need to identify m6A modification sites from RNA sequences for specific tissues (brain, liver, kidney, heart, testis) in human, mouse, or rat, starting from FASTA sequences or the 11 benchmark datasets (h_b, h_k, h_l, m_b, m_h, m_k, m_l, m_t, r_b, r_k, r_l). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-m6a-site-prediction))
- **rna-modification-call-filtering**: Use when reprocessing ONT POD5 files from direct RNA (or cDNA/DNA) runs requires basecalling with Dorado, mapping with minimap2, and modkit extraction/filtering of modified-base calls into filtered BED/bedMethyl outputs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/rna-modification-call-filtering))
- **rna-modification-co-occurrence-analysis**: Use when you have per-read modification predictions for two modification types (e.g. CHEUI-solo m6A and m5C calls) on the same direct RNA reads and want to test single-molecule co-occurrence at pairs of modified sites 1–15 nt apart. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/rna-modification-co-occurrence-analysis))
- **rna-modification-ks-sample-compare-testing**: Use when raw ionic-current signal from native and IVT-control direct RNA nanopore reads (multi-fast5 converted to per-read fast5) must be compared to localize endogenous 2'-O-methyl or base modifications per nucleotide. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/rna-modification-ks-sample-compare-testing))
- **rna-modification-site-annotation**: Use when raw per-tool m6A caller outputs (e.g. Nanom6A probabilities, Yanocomp/DiffErr FDR, DRUMMER p-values, EpiNano error sums) must be converted to a common genomic BED format for cross-tool comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/rna-modification-site-annotation))
- **rna-modification-site-calling**: Use when you have Nanopore direct RNA FAST5 signals (RNA002) and need transcriptome-wide per-read and per-site m6A or m5C calls without sequence-context constraints, including stoichiometry at transcript reference sites. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/rna-modification-site-calling))
- **rna-modification-site-counting-per-replicate**: Use when filtered modkit BED site tables from multiple nanopore direct RNA replicates are available and the goal is per-replicate site counts plus cross-replicate shared-site counts at a specific gene locus (e.g., Pkm in mouse C2C12 dRNA data). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/rna-modification-site-counting-per-replicate))
- **rna-modification-stoichiometry-aggregation**: Use when you have modification-aware basecalled dRNA reads (pod5/fast5 basecalled to fasta/fastq with mod indexes in headers, aligned to sorted BAM) and need per-read plus aggregated modification stoichiometry. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-modification-stoichiometry-aggregation))
- **rna-modification-stoichiometry-quantification**: Use when nanopore dRNA-Seq fast5 reads must be converted into per-site modification stoichiometry values (modRatio) for m6A sites, e.g., to compare HEK293 WT vs METTL3-KO profiles or to regress measured stoichiometry against known titration fractions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/rna-modification-stoichiometry-quantification))
- **rna-profile-cluster-ground-truth-labeling**: Use when benchmarking HTO-based demultiplexing methods on a cell hashing dataset where HTO counts alone cannot be trusted as truth, and RNA profiles are available to establish droplet identities independently. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-profile-cluster-ground-truth-labeling))
- **rna-pseudouridine-site-dataset-construction**: Use when building positive/negative Ψ-site training and test sets from RMBase v2.0 annotations that require sequence-identity reduction across 10-fold splits, e.g. for evaluating RNA modification predictors under balanced and imbalanced class ratios. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-pseudouridine-site-dataset-construction))
- **rna-quantification**: Workflow for gene and transcript quantification from RNA-seq reads using alignment-based or alignment-free tools. - **rna-quantification-alignment-free-quant**: Quantify transcript expression using pseudo-alignment with Salmon or kallisto. Use when quantifying transcripts with Salmon or kallisto. - **rna-quantification-count-matrix-qc**: Quality control and exploration of RNA-seq count matrices before differential expression. Check for outliers, batch effects, and sample relationships. - **rna-quantification-featurecounts-counting**: Count reads per gene from aligned BAM files using Subread featureCounts. - **rna-quantification-tximport-workflow**: Import transcript-level quantifications from Salmon/kallisto into R for gene-level analysis with DESeq2/edgeR using tximport or tximeta.
