- **rna-read-sequence-classification**: Use when you have unmapped RNAseq reads (e.g., *_unmapped.fastq after removal of hg19/phiX alignments) and need an alignment-free binary viral-vs-human read classifier or predicted viral contigs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-read-sequence-classification))
- **rna-reference-transcript-preparation**: Use when single-read FAST5 direct RNA reads must be raw-signal-aligned against a reference transcript FASTA (rRNA sequences) so per-read event tables exist for Tombo detect_modifications and extract_array.py. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/rna-reference-transcript-preparation))
- **rna-reverse-transcription**: RNA 反转录合成 cDNA：全式金 AU341 试剂盒、对照设置、实验准备和周期估算。 ([biojiayuxin/potato-agent](https://github.com/biojiayuxin/potato-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/potato-wet-lab-protocols/rna-reverse-transcription) · ★1)
- **rna-secondary-structure-feature-encoding**: Use when building deep learning inputs for RNA site prediction where secondary structure context should complement sequence, e.g. 51 nt fragments centered on a uridine/Ψ site. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-secondary-structure-feature-encoding))
- **rna-secondary-structure-free-energy-estimation**: Use when per-tool TP/FP/FN m6A bin classifications from dRNA-seq benchmarking are available and the goal is to find sequence features (GC content, folding free energy, sequence entropy) that distinguish error classes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/rna-secondary-structure-free-energy-estimation))
- **rna-secondary-structure-masking**: Use when predicting RNA:dsDNA triplex interactions with 3plex/Triplexator-style scanning and you want to exclude ssRNA nucleotides unlikely to be available for Hoogsteen pairing, or when benchmarking whether structure-aware masking improves TPX prediction AUC for lncRNAs with validated binding. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/rna-secondary-structure-masking))
- **rna-secondary-structure-prediction**: Use when single-molecule SHAPE reactivity profiles (nanoSHAPE or SHAPE-MaP) for a specific RNA are available and the goal is to test whether constrained folding recovers expected structural elements (e.g., miRNA hairpins). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/rna-secondary-structure-prediction))
- **rna-seq-abundance-matrix-handling**: Use when you have transcript-level quantification output files (quant.sf, quant.gz) from salmon, kallisto, sailfish, or oarfish and need to produce gene-level count matrices, abundance matrices, and length-based offsets for differential expression analysis in DESeq2, edgeR, or limma-voom. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v1/skills/rna-seq-abundance-matrix-handling))
- **rna-seq-adapter-quality-trimming**: Use when raw RNA-seq FASTQ files (e.g., pESC/aESC reads retrieved from SRA per Supplemental Table S1) must be adapter- and quality-trimmed before alignment to GRCh38/hg38 with STAR and expression quantification with RSEM/DESeq2. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-adapter-quality-trimming))
- **rna-seq-alignment-feature-extraction**: Use when you have coordinate-sorted RNA-seq BAM alignments plus RefSeq GTF annotation and need to classify candidate terminal exons (skipped vs composite IPA) using read-based features rather than sequence motifs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-alignment-feature-extraction))
- **rna-seq-alignment-grch38**: Use when the input is human (GRCh38/hg38) RNA-seq FASTQ reads that must be aligned to a RefSeq-annotated genome before IPA site prediction with InPACT or downstream transcript quantification with Salmon. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-alignment-grch38))
- **rna-seq-alignment-quality-assessment**: Use when RNA-seq FASTQ files from multiple tissue groups must be aligned and you need per-sample STAR alignment statistics (unique mapping %, unmapped 'too short' %, chimeric %) to compare alignment quality across groups before ASE or expression analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-alignment-quality-assessment))
- **rna-seq-alignment-quality-control**: Use when raw RNA-seq reads (e.g. the six untreated/LPS-activated monocyte samples from GSE118165) must be aligned to GRCh38 and cleaned into coordinate-sorted BAMs before InPACT can predict novel IPA sites. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-alignment-quality-control))
- **rna-seq-analysis**: Bulk RNA-seq analysis pipeline covering alignment (STAR), quantification (Salmon, featureCounts), and differential expression (DESeq2). Triggers on RNA-seq, STAR, Salmon, DESeq2, differential expression, gene expression, tximport, featureCounts, transcriptomics, "bulk RNA-seq", "STAR alignment", "Salmon quantification", "gene counts", "DESeq2 results", "shrinkage estimator", "volcano plot RNA-seq". ([awslabs/hcls-agent-skills](https://github.com/awslabs/hcls-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/rna-seq-analysis) · ★76)
- **rna-seq-and-splicing**: Use when analyzing bulk RNA-seq in any organism: strandedness, QC, quantification, differential expression, splicing, fusions, or human rare-disease outliers. Not for assay planning (sample-and-assay-planning). ([oumarkme/research_agents](https://github.com/oumarkme/research_agents/tree/HEAD/plugins/bioinfo-lab/skills/rna-seq-and-splicing))
- **rna-seq-bam-preprocessing-and-merging**: Use when you have mapped RNA-seq reads (bulk or merged single-cell BAM) and need a BAF shift profile without a heterozygous variant call set, e.g. to supply CaSpER's allelic-shift signal. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-bam-preprocessing-and-merging))
- **rna-seq-bias-quantification**: Use when RNA-Seq expression estimates (e.g. FPKM from Cufflinks or eXpress) show poor agreement across technical replicate libraries or against qRT-PCR, and sequence/positional fragment bias is suspected as the cause. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-bias-quantification))
- **rna-seq-cancer-normal-classification**: Use when you have cancer and normal RNA-seq expression matrices (bulk or single-cell) plus a fixed gene set and need to benchmark its generalization accuracy on independent GEO datasets with a neural-network classifier. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-cancer-normal-classification))
- **rna-seq-count-data-simulation**: Use when you need simulated gene-by-sample RNA-seq count matrices with known differential-expression ground truth to benchmark DEG detection methods such as DESeq2, edgeR, limma, or NOISeq. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-count-data-simulation))
- **rna-seq-count-filtering-and-normalization**: Use when raw count matrices (e.g. MOSim-simulated RNA-seq or miRNA-seq counts, or experimental bulk counts) must be filtered for low-expression features and TMM-normalized before differential expression analysis or regulatory-network inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/rna-seq-count-filtering-and-normalization))
- **rna-seq-count-filtering-normalization**: Use when raw or simulated RNA-seq count matrices (e.g. MOSim mosim() bulk output) must be filtered and normalized prior to limma/voom linear modeling, DE testing, or GRN-inference preprocessing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-count-filtering-normalization))
- **rna-seq-count-matrix-alignment**: Use when loading expression count data plus a sample metadata/design table (e.g. BRCA_normCounts and BRCA_metadata from DEGGs) prior to generate_subnetworks or any per-sample subgroup analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-count-matrix-alignment))
- **rna-seq-count-matrix-generation**: Use when you have transcript-level abundance estimates from salmon, sailfish, or kallisto quantification and need gene-level count matrices for differential expression analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v1/skills/rna-seq-count-matrix-generation))
- **rna-seq-count-matrix-import**: Use when the input is a raw/normalized RNA-seq count matrix (csv/txt, genes in rows or first column, one column per sample) and the goal is to run differential expression via DESeq2, optionally with a metadata table of experimental factors. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/rna-seq-count-matrix-import))
- **rna-seq-count-matrix-preparation**: Use when fitting SNP–gene eQTL or differential expression models with kimma's kmFit/kmFit_eQTL and you must assemble expression counts, sample metadata, and a kinship matrix into the expected dat/kin/counts/meta format. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-count-matrix-preparation))
- **rna-seq-count-normalization**: Use when you have transcript-level abundance estimates and inferred counts from salmon/kallisto/Sailfish (with or without Gibbs/bootstrap replicates) that must be summarized to gene or transcript level, and you plan to use count-based differential expression tools (edgeR, DESeq2, limma-voom). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v1/skills/rna-seq-count-normalization))
- **rna-seq-count-normalization-deseq2**: Use when starting from raw RNA-seq gene count data across contrasting conditions (e.g. perturbation vs. vehicle control) that must be put on a comparable scale before downstream signature or pathway-activity scoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-count-normalization-deseq2))
- **rna-seq-count-normalization-tmm**: Use when apply TMM normalization when you have raw RNA-seq read counts from multiple samples and suspect differences in library composition (e.g., one sample over-represents a highly-expressed gene or transcript class relative to others). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v1/skills/rna-seq-count-normalization-tmm))
- **rna-seq-count-statistics**: Use when you have raw TCGA RNA-Seq gene count matrices split into normal vs cancer samples (e.g. KIRC, LIHC, PRAD) and need a DE-gene baseline signature to benchmark a novel biomarker-extraction method. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-count-statistics))
- **rna-seq-count-table-normalization**: Use when raw RNA-Seq count tables (e.g. DREAM 4 In Silico time-series or C. beijerinckii NRRL B-598 data) must be normalized with CPM, RPKM, or TPM prior to mutual-information GRN inference, or when benchmarking normalization effects on network metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-count-table-normalization))
- **rna-seq-coverage-classification**: Use when you need to assess how accurately predicted splice junction coverage (e.g., MRSD scores) matches achieved coverage in independent RNA-seq samples, using PPV/NPV as validation metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-coverage-classification))
- **rna-seq-coverage-decay-quantification**: Use when base-level pileup coverage from an RNA-seq cohort must be screened for degraded RNA — e.g. samples with steep 3'-to-5' coverage slopes that would masquerade as spurious shape-change outliers in downstream analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-coverage-decay-quantification))
- **rna-seq-coverage-feature-engineering**: Use when training or reproducing aptardi Mode 2 models that need RNA-Seq coverage (alone or with DNA sequence) as features over 3' terminal exons for polyadenylation-site prediction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-coverage-feature-engineering))
- **rna-seq-coverage-variance-stabilization**: Use when raw base-level RNA-seq coverage pileup matrices are skewed and must be variance-stabilized (log-transform with tuned pseudo-count) prior to SCISSOR global/local shape change detection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-coverage-variance-stabilization))
- **rna-seq-data-processing-execution**: Use when raw FASTQ RNA-seq data (e.g. WT versus KO designs from GEO) must be processed end-to-end into QC reports, BAM files, bigWig tracks, and differential gene expression results with a reproducible, low-input pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-data-processing-execution))
- **rna-seq-dataset-curation**: Use when building a curated tumor/non-tumor RNA-Seq expression matrix from the combined Wang et al. TCGA/GTEx resource and packaging it as a shareable MOG project with metadata. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/rna-seq-dataset-curation))
- **rna-seq-de-novo-transcript-assembly**: Use when the goal is to reconstruct full IGHV-D-J Ig transcripts from an RNA-seq BAM (e.g., for IGHV hypermutation status in CLL) rather than inferring the B-cell receptor repertoire from only a portion of the IGHV gene segment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-de-novo-transcript-assembly))
- **rna-seq-de-novo-transcriptome-assembly**: Use when you have Illumina paired-end RNA-seq reads (e.g. 2x100bp FASTQ, possibly with 10x/barcode structure) and need reference-free recovery of full-length isoforms from high-isoform-diversity genes like GYPC, MAPT, or BIN1. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-de-novo-transcriptome-assembly))
- **rna-seq-degradation-artifact**: Use when a sample fakes low pathway scores in RNA-seq QC. ([ShiganLuo/skill](https://github.com/ShiganLuo/skill/tree/HEAD/bioinformatics/rna-seq-degradation-artifact))
- **rna-seq-differential-expression-analysis**: Use when FASTQ RNA-seq reads (with replicates across conditions) must be turned into per-method differential expression result tables and the Watchdog WMS is available, with bwa and ContextMap2 installed and configured. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-differential-expression-analysis))
- **rna-seq-differential-expression-analysis**: Use when raw RNA-seq data (SRA run accessions or FASTQ files) or a gene/miRNA/isoform count matrix must be turned into a ranked list of differentially expressed features between phenotypes (e.g. TNBC vs other BC subtypes) as part of a multi-omics workflow. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/rna-seq-differential-expression-analysis))
- **rna-seq-differential-expression-analysis**: Use when you have a raw RNA-seq count matrix plus sample metadata with a defined experimental design and need robust DEG calls that are not biased by one method's assumptions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-differential-expression-analysis--864ee06faecb526b))
- **rna-seq-differential-expression-analysis**: Use when a raw RNA-seq counts matrix (.csv, Ensembl ID rows, sample ID columns) must be tested for differentially expressed genes between sample groups, or when edgeR/limma/DESeq2 runtimes must be benchmarked on identical input. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-differential-expression-analysis--731bf50ae1c8b81d))
- **rna-seq-differential-expression-analysis**: Use when a normalized RNA-seq expression matrix with two-group labels (e.g., 46 critical vs 23 non-critical COVID-19 cases from SEMdata) must be turned into per-gene DEG P-values to seed network analyses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-differential-expression-analysis--5c194ecb897d7a2e))
- **rna-seq-differential-expression-analysis**: Use when modeling RNA-seq gene counts from paired/related sample designs where a genetic kinship matrix is available, and you need per-gene DEG calls plus AIC-based comparison of models with and without the kinship covariance random effect. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-differential-expression-analysis--1d9363f96e5e55b4))
- **rna-seq-differential-expression-analysis**: Use when you have RNA-seq read count data (from alignment tools, transcript quantification, or feature counting) organized in a count matrix with samples as columns and genes as rows, paired with sample metadata (condition, batch, treatment), and you want to test which genes show statistically. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v1/skills/rna-seq-differential-expression-analysis))
- **rna-seq-dimensionality-reduction**: Use when you have a gene-by-cell RNA-seq expression matrix (thousands of genes) with batch labels and need a lower-dimensional embedding that preserves batch-specific variation for batch effect detection/quantification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-dimensionality-reduction))
- **rna-seq-exon-count-simulation-from-population-parameters**: Use when benchmarking an alternative-splicing or ASAS detection method against competitors and you need simulated paired allelic count data with controlled variance and replicate number instead of real RNA-seq reads. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-exon-count-simulation-from-population-parameters))
- **rna-seq-experimental-design-specification**: Use when before constructing a DESeqDataSet from count data or tximport output, when you have raw RNA-seq samples that need to be annotated with experimental conditions, treatment groups, batch effects, or other covariates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v1/skills/rna-seq-experimental-design-specification))
- **rna-seq-expression-alignment-across-cells**: Use when you have paired scATAC-seq and scRNA-seq data from the same cells (multiome experiment) and want to perform integrated analysis that leverages both chromatin accessibility and gene expression signals. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/rna-seq-expression-alignment-across-cells))
- **rna-seq-expression-based-gene-stratification**: Use when you need to partition a gene set into expression-based classes (e.g. three groups of 100 highly/moderately/silently expressed RefSeq genes) from RNA-seq data to relate them to ChIP-seq signal levels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-expression-based-gene-stratification))
- **rna-seq-expression-classification**: Use when you need to evaluate whether genes from extracted active subnetworks discriminate between two patient groups (case/control) and to compare classifier performance (accuracy, predictor gene set size) across subnetwork detection methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-expression-classification))
- **rna-seq-expression-data-preprocessing**: Use when raw TCGA RNA-Seq gene expression matrices must be filtered and normalized before computing gene co-expression networks, and only protein-coding/lncRNA genes with sufficient expression (mean zFPKM &gt; -3.0) should be kept. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-expression-data-preprocessing))
- **rna-seq-expression-estimation**: Use when RNA-Seq alignments (SAM) and a reference (FASTA/GTF annotation) must yield expression estimates whose fragment sequence, priming, or position bias is learned from the data rather than assumed uniform, e.g. MAQC HBR or strand-specific SRA020818 yeast libraries. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-expression-estimation))
- **rna-seq-expression-feature-representation**: Use when a TCGA-style RNA-seq expression matrix with five intrinsic subtype labels (Luminal A/B, Basal, Her2-enriched, Normal-like) must be turned into a classifier feature space for supervised single-sample subtyping. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-expression-feature-representation))
- **rna-seq-expression-matrix-loading**: Use when you have a raw RNA-seq expression matrix (genes or transcripts x samples, e.g. ENCODE matrices with ~100 samples) that must be formatted, cleaned, and loaded into PyWGCNA before co-expression module identification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-expression-matrix-loading))
- **rna-seq-expression-matrix-preprocessing**: Use when raw TCGA RNA-seq data for multiple cancer types must be assembled into a single tumor/normal gene-expression matrix before feature selection or classification, e.g. to compute per-gene DI scores over 20,501 genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-expression-matrix-preprocessing))
- **rna-seq-expression-matrix-processing**: Use when a pre-trained RNA-seq subtyping model (mLR with PAM50, limma50, or limma50_BWE features) must be applied to a wholly unseen external expression matrix, or when reproducing external testing accuracies like Table 2. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-expression-matrix-processing--43c00ffe3bbb25e9))
- **rna-seq-expression-matrix-processing**: Use when the input is raw RNA-Seq data from a high-dimensional transcriptomics experiment (P &gt;&gt; n, e.g. ArrayExpress E-MTAB-11611) that must be converted into a predictor matrix x and outcome vector y for glmnet/nestedcv modelling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-expression-matrix-processing--7159291fed82b9ae))
- **rna-seq-fastq-download-from-geo-ena**: Use when raw RNA-seq reads for a published study must be fetched from public archives — GSE accessions via GEO/SRA or a BioProject via direct ENA run downloads — before transcript-level quantification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-fastq-download-from-geo-ena))
- **rna-seq-gene-id-mapping**: Use when the input expression matrix is a .csv counts file with Ensembl gene IDs as row names and TCGA-style sample IDs as columns, and downstream GSVA, integration, or correlation analysis requires gene-symbol-keyed TPM data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-gene-id-mapping))
- **rna-seq-linear-mixed-effects-modeling**: Use when RNA-seq differential expression data contain paired or repeated samples from the same donor (e.g., pre/post or within-patient designs) and you need per-gene mixed-model P-values plus AIC/BIC fit metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-linear-mixed-effects-modeling))
- **rna-seq-outlier-sample-exclusion**: Use when reproducing a published RNA-seq differential expression analysis whose original authors identified an outlier sample via QA/QC, or when PCA/box-plot diagnostics reveal a mislabeled or poorly separated sample before fitting a DE model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-outlier-sample-exclusion))
- **rna-seq-paired-end-read-simulation**: Use when benchmarking a circRNA full-length assembly or isoform quantification method against known ground truth, requiring simulated paired-end reads with controlled depth, read length, and per-condition isoform abundances. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-paired-end-read-simulation))
- **rna-seq-pileup-shape-change-interpretation**: Use when a list of publicly reported splice-site mutation calls (e.g. TCGA HNSC MAF-style calls for TP53) must be cross-checked against SCISSOR shape-change outliers to flag likely mis-annotated calls. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-pileup-shape-change-interpretation))
- **rna-seq-pileup-table-preparation**: Use when sorted RNAseq BAM files must be converted into position-by-position variant/invariant nucleotide frequency tables (REDItools outTables, bgzip+tabix indexed) to feed REDInet or other RNA editing classifiers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-pileup-table-preparation))
- **rna-seq-preprocessing-and-normalization**: Use when expression data (microarray, bulk or single-cell RNA-seq, e.g. TCGA-like sample-by-gene matrices) has a non-normal, bimodal, or multimodal feature distribution and must be Z-score normalized before correlation analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-preprocessing-and-normalization))
- **rna-seq-preprocessing-pipeline-orchestration**: Use when when you have raw FASTQ files (from SRA or local storage) and need to produce normalized transcript quantification (quant.sf) files for a multi-sample RNA-seq cohort. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-preprocessing-pipeline-orchestration))
- **rna-seq-quantification-selective-alignment**: Use when you have paired-end or single-end RNA-seq reads (FASTQ format) and a reference transcriptome (FASTA), and you need to estimate transcript-level abundances (NumReads and TPM per transcript) rather than gene-level counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v1/skills/rna-seq-quantification-selective-alignment))
- **rna-seq-quantification-validation**: Use when you have quantification output from two versions or implementations of the same tool (e.g., C++ vs. Rust rewrites, pre- and post-bugfix releases) and need to verify that they produce equivalent results before deploying the new version. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v1/skills/rna-seq-quantification-validation))
- **rna-seq-read-alignment**: Use when you have raw FASTQ reads (paired-end, e.g., first-strand Illumina libraries) and need a sorted, genome-aligned BAM as input to downstream transcriptome reconstruction (StringTie) or aptardi polyA-site prediction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-read-alignment--6aeb6356002f863f))
- **rna-seq-read-alignment**: Use when raw paired-end RNA-seq FASTQs from chicken (or other species) must be spliced-aligned for downstream per-sample variant calling (g.vcf/HaplotypeCaller) or allele-specific expression analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-read-alignment--38e2cf87f644b5cf))
- **rna-seq-read-alignment-gene-counting**: Use when raw scRNA-seq FASTQ data (e.g. ArrayExpress E-MTAB-6854 droplet-prepared mouse epithelial libraries) must be converted into a per-gene exonic read-pair count matrix for each demultiplexed sample combination. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-read-alignment-gene-counting))
- **rna-seq-read-alignment-multi-aligner**: Use when you need to demonstrate that a mapping-derived signal (e.g. a shift, bias, or clustering pattern) is algorithm-independent by repeating the same alignment on identical input FASTQ with multiple aligners and comparing downstream distributions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-read-alignment-multi-aligner))
- **rna-seq-read-alignment-star**: Use when preprocessing labeled SPLiT-seq FASTQ reads (e.g., SCSit output with combinatorial barcodes and UMI) need to be aligned to mm10, hg19, or a combined mm10-hg19 reference to compare mapped-read yields. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-read-alignment-star))
- **rna-seq-read-count-aggregation-per-node**: Use when a KEGG pathway node contains multiple functionally homologous genes (e.g., the 4-gene ALDH3A1 node in hsa00340 or the 21-gene PLA2G4B node in hsa00564) and you need one node-level read count and regulation value instead of per-gene ambiguity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-read-count-aggregation-per-node))
- **rna-seq-read-depth-subsampling**: Use when benchmarking fusion-gene detection sensitivity (or similar read-count-dependent callers) across decreasing sequencing depths from a single deeply sequenced paired-end FASTQ sample. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-read-depth-subsampling))
- **rna-seq-read-fragmentation**: Use when training or fairly benchmarking fixed-length read classifiers (e.g., viRNAtrap vs DeepVirFinder, DeepViFi, ViraMiner, Seq2Seq) and you need viral and human transcripts converted into comparable 48 bp fragments with shared train/test splits. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-read-fragmentation))
- **rna-seq-read-mapping-parameter-tuning**: Use when when comparing two implementations of the same RNA-seq mapping algorithm on identical reference indices and read sets, if per-read mapping agreement is &lt;99.8% or the overall mapping rate differs by &gt;0. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v1/skills/rna-seq-read-mapping-parameter-tuning))
- **rna-seq-read-pseudoalignment-quantification**: Use when you need to quantify reads supporting annotated and novel splicing events from short-read RNA-seq FASTQ files faster than a spliced aligner (fortuna was ~7x faster and mapped reads with mismatches across novel junctions more accurately). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-read-pseudoalignment-quantification))
- **rna-seq-read-quantification**: Use when raw FASTQ RNA-seq reads from a model or non-model eukaryote must be converted to a KO-level count table, especially when no reference transcriptome exists or when cross-version equivalence with Kallisto-based quantification must be checked. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-read-quantification))
- **rna-seq-read-realignment-to-masked-genome**: Use when per-allele read counts at heterozygous biallelic SNPs show inflated reference allele ratios (e.g. ~51.8–51.9% instead of the expected 50%) and you suspect the reference genome biases alignment toward the reference allele. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-read-realignment-to-masked-genome))
- **rna-seq-read-simulation**: Use when benchmarking junction/splicing-event detection accuracy on controlled data: you need simulated FASTQs with known held-out novel junctions and per-event-type recall/precision ground truth. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-read-simulation))
- **rna-seq-read-simulation-from-count-matrices**: Use when you need simulated RNA-seq FASTQ data whose underlying expression counts contain a known, controlled differential expression pattern for teaching or validating DESeq2/Salmon workflows. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/rna-seq-read-simulation-from-count-matrices))
- **rna-seq-read-simulation-with-rsem**: Use when a benchmark requires a ground-truth RNA-Seq dataset with known transcript provenance, so assembler completeness (95%-assembled genes/isoforms), accuracy (misassemblies), and duplication ratio can be evaluated against a reference transcriptome. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/rna-seq-read-simulation-with-rsem))
- **rna-seq-read-truncation-processing**: Use when you need to generate derivative sequencing-length variants (single-end truncations of paired-end FASTQ reads) to test robustness of an expression quantification metric across sequencing lengths and strategies. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-read-truncation-processing))
- **rna-seq-regulon-gene-list-processing**: Use when two or more differentially expressed gene sets (e.g. H-NS and LuxR regulons from RNA-seq comparisons) must be intersected to count shared regulators/genes, with identifiers harmonized first. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-regulon-gene-list-processing))
- **rna-seq-replicate-reproducibility-analysis**: Use when you have read counts or rank lists for the same transcript/gene set from two replicate RNA-seq experiments (within-lab or between-lab, e.g. SEQC comparisons) and need to quantify reproducibility with error control at a target mFDR such as alpha = 0.01. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/rna-seq-replicate-reproducibility-analysis))
- **rna-seq-sample-outlier-detection**: Use when an RNA-Seq or expression dataset with sample metadata (condition, organ, tumor status) needs a QC pass to confirm that within-condition samples are consistent and to flag outlier samples before differential expression analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/rna-seq-sample-outlier-detection))
- **rna-seq-sequencing-depth-quantification**: Use when you need gene- and tissue-specific sequencing depth predictions (in millions of reads) for splice junction coverage of a gene panel, e.g. deciding which GTEx biosample and depth to use for RNA-seq mis-splicing surveillance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-sequencing-depth-quantification))
- **rna-seq-shape-change-annotation**: Use when SCISSOR has flagged shape-change outliers (e.g., ScissorOutput$GSC / $LSC sample IDs) and you need to determine which detected aberrations correspond to existing mutation calls versus novel events. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-shape-change-annotation))
- **rna-seq-short-read-alignment**: Use when aligning RNA-Seq FASTQ files inside a systemPipeR workflow and you need to choose or benchmark an aligner (Bowtie2/TopHat2 vs HISAT2/Rsubread) or verify near-linear parallel speedup via clusterRun. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-short-read-alignment--e6aaa64bf61235cd))
- **rna-seq-short-read-alignment**: Use when FASTQ RNA-Seq reads (single- or paired-end Illumina) must be spliced-aligned to a reference genome before expression estimation, and novel junction discovery should be suppressed in favor of known annotated junctions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-short-read-alignment--fc0d5c051446688c))
- **rna-seq-signature-count-augmentation**: Use when an RNA-seq quantification based on fortuna signature counts (cnt.tsv/alt.tsv from --quant) must account for novel splice junctions that an aligner like STAR found in previously unmapped reads. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-signature-count-augmentation))
- **rna-seq-spanning-read-cluster-analysis**: Use when RNA-Seq read support at a fusion locus forms multiple spanning-read junction clusters and one must decide which cluster is the primary fusion junction versus alternative-splice or backsplice junctions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-spanning-read-cluster-analysis))
- **rna-seq-splice-event-quantification**: Use when you need to detect and count novel alternative splicing events (ES, IR, AA, AD, AP, IE) from RNA-seq FASTQs using only a GTF annotation and genome FASTA, without a spliced aligner, or when tissue-specific event catalogs (e.g. RPM &gt;= 1) are needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-splice-event-quantification))
- **rna-seq-spliced-read-alignment**: Use when paired-end RNA-seq FASTQ files must be spliced-aligned before circRNA full-length assembly, e.g. SRA/GSA reads destined for CircAST, CIRCexplorer2/CIRI2/UROBORUS junction detection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-spliced-read-alignment--28effd6b3cacaa34))
- **rna-seq-spliced-read-alignment**: Use when raw RNA-seq FASTQ files must be converted into per-gene read counts (e.g., for the Prensner PCa cohort from dbGap phs000443.v1.p1) prior to differential expression or pathway-node aggregation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-spliced-read-alignment--e3575dfd69f5ded0))
- **rna-seq-sra-read-acquisition**: Use when the starting point is NCBI SRA accession numbers (SRR/SRX) for the paper's single-organism RNA-Seq datasets and paired-end FASTQ files are needed to launch rnaSPAdes, SPAdes, Trinity, or other transcriptome assemblers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/rna-seq-sra-read-acquisition))
- **rna-seq-statistical-filtering**: Use when a counts matrix plus a two-group factor (e.g. hypoxia-high vs hypoxia-low TCGA-LUAD samples) must be tested for differentially expressed genes and filtered at explicit p/logFC/FDR cut-offs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-statistical-filtering))
- **rna-seq-tmm-normalization-log2-cpm**: Use when you have raw gene-level counts (e.g. kimma's example.count) plus sample metadata (example.dat) and want per-gene unpaired linear-model DEG testing on TMM-normalized log2 CPM expression with fit metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-tmm-normalization-log2-cpm))
- **rna-seq-tpm-expression-retrieval**: Use when building a soybean–fungal infection expression dataset (C. gregata, F. graminearum, F. virguliforme, M. phaseolina, P. pachyrhizi) from public TPM estimates to feed coexpression or differential analyses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-tpm-expression-retrieval))
- **rna-seq-tpm-integration**: Use when computing CENTRE features for enhancer-target pairs and a TPM expression table must be attached to each target gene before calling centrePrediction, or when checking that RNA-seq inputs match CENTRE's expected schema. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-tpm-integration))
- **rna-seq-transcript-abundance-quantification**: Use when starting from RNA-seq FASTQ files (e.g. Polyester-simulated paired-end reads) and transcript-level abundance estimates or a gene-level count matrix are needed for differential expression analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/rna-seq-transcript-abundance-quantification))
- **rna-seq-transcript-assembly**: Use when you have splice-aware BAM alignments of RNA-seq reads from one or more aligners (e.g., Hisat2 and Star) mapped to a reference genome and need to assemble transcripts, especially lowly expressed ones missed by single-aligner assemblers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-transcript-assembly--7c861c3687177fbc))
- **rna-seq-transcript-assembly**: Use when raw RNA-Seq FASTQ samples (e.g., 180 TARGET osteosarcoma tumors plus 9 normal bone controls) must be converted into a consensus transcript candidate set prior to novel lncRNA detection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-transcript-assembly--919b7e7a98d7da41))
- **rna-seq-transcript-quantification**: Use when raw RNA-seq FASTQ files from GEO/ENA (disease and control samples) must be converted to per-sample isoform-level TPM abundances as input for isoform switching / dMDT calling in the CanIsoNet workflow. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-transcript-quantification))
- **rna-seq-transcriptome-assembly-benchmarking**: Use when comparing de novo transcriptome assemblers across datasets with different gene-detection depths, especially when raw counts of assembled genes are unfair and a Kallisto-normalized fraction of 95%-assembled genes is needed for cross-dataset ranking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/rna-seq-transcriptome-assembly-benchmarking))
- **rna-seq-treatment-response-classification**: Use when classifying binary treatment response (e.g. rituximab responders vs non-responders in the R4RA E-MTAB-11611 cohort) from transcriptomic matrices where predictors vastly outnumber samples (P &gt;&gt; n) and an honest out-of-sample AUC is required. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-treatment-response-classification))
- **rna-seq-workflow-environment-generation**: Use when a user needs to rapidly test or run a complete pre-configured RNA-Seq NGS workflow in R/Bioconductor, from environment generation through execution to HTML scientific reporting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/rna-seq-workflow-environment-generation))
- **rna-sequence-error-rate-quantification**: Use when aligned ONT direct RNA reads (BAM) against an mRNA reference are available and you need a total error rate plus mismatch/deletion/insertion decomposition, or to compare dRNA-specific per-nucleotide error biases against matched plasmid/cDNA sequencing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/rna-sequence-error-rate-quantification))
- **rna-sequence-feature-computation**: Use when per-tool TP/FP/FN bin classifications from an m6A dRNA-seq benchmark need to be explained by sequence context (motif variant, GC, structure, entropy). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/rna-sequence-feature-computation))
- **rna-sequence-feature-extraction**: Use when raw RNA sequences (fixed length, e.g. 41 nt FASTA or CSV) must be converted into position-specific k-mer/gapped k-mer binary feature vectors for ML-based modification-site prediction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-sequence-feature-extraction--9c5eb6a147ad77bb))
- **rna-sequence-feature-extraction**: Use when raw RNA transcript sequences in FASTA format must be converted into the numeric feature set (SIFs, SS k-mer scores, ORF, protein features) that LncDC's XGBoost model consumes for lncRNA prediction, or when reproducing its SSF ablation experiments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-sequence-feature-extraction--bb0da9d4d3d83c72))
- **rna-sequence-identity-reduction**: Use when building train/test splits of RNA fragments (e.g., 51-nt pseudouridine-site windows) where homologous sequences across folds would inflate prediction accuracy through leakage. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-sequence-identity-reduction))
- **rna-sequence-one-hot-encoding**: Use when preparing Ψ-centered 51-bp RNA fragments (sequence plus RNAshapes dot-bracket structure) from RMBase v2.0 as fixed-size one-of-K binary matrices to feed paired 1D CNN inputs like MU-PseUDeep. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-sequence-one-hot-encoding--86d733f2d06272a8))
- **rna-sequence-one-hot-encoding**: Use when raw RNA sequence segments (e.g., 41-nt tissue-specific m6A positive/negative samples) must be converted into fixed-length numeric feature matrices before feeding a CNN or other fixed-input neural network model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-sequence-one-hot-encoding--488e8f697ca26b0e))
- **rna-structural-content-figure-generation**: Use when you have an RNA secondary structure in CT (connectivity table) or dot-bracket FASTA format plus a tab-delimited per-nucleotide Shannon entropy file, and you need BPC/entropy plots and structure-count heatmap/histogram outputs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-structural-content-figure-generation))
- **rna-structure-basepair-content-filtering**: Use when you have per-window BPC values and smoothed Shannon entropy values for an RNA (e.g., from HCV_Jc1 CT/dot-bracket structure plus a shannon.txt) and must designate structure counts under RSCanner's dual-threshold filtering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-structure-basepair-content-filtering))
- **rna-structure-lineplot-visualization**: Use when you have an RNA secondary structure (CT or dot-bracket format) plus a per-nucleotide Shannon entropy file and need per-nucleotide BPC and smoothed SE line plots (e.g. reproducing Supplementary Fig. S2A/B for HCV_Jc1). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-structure-lineplot-visualization))
- **rna-structure-ncrna-search**: Searches for non-coding RNA homologs and classifies RNA families using Infernal covariance model searches against the Rfam database. Identifies structured RNAs by sequence and secondary structure conservation. Use when querying sequences against Rfam, building custom covariance models for novel RNA families, or classifying non-coding transcripts by family. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/rna-structure-ncrna-search) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **rna-structure-secondary-structure-prediction**: Predicts RNA secondary structures using minimum free energy folding and partition function analysis with ViennaRNA (RNAfold, RNAalifold, RNAcofold). Computes base-pair probabilities, centroid structures, and consensus structures from alignments. Use when predicting RNA folding, evaluating structural stability, or comparing structures across homologs. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/rna-structure-secondary-structure-prediction) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **rna-structure-structure-probing**: Analyzes experimental RNA structure probing data from SHAPE-MaP and DMS-MaPseq experiments using ShapeMapper2. Converts mutation rates to per-nucleotide reactivity profiles that constrain structure prediction. Use when processing SHAPE-MaP or DMS-MaPseq sequencing data to obtain experimental RNA structure information. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/rna-structure-structure-probing) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **rna-transcript-abundance-simulation**: Use when simulating ONT direct-RNA (RNA004) nanopore signals from a transcriptome where per-transcript abundance and truncation profiles must be honored, e.g. reproducing HG002-RNA004 experiments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/rna-transcript-abundance-simulation))
- **rna-transcript-dataset-construction**: Use when building training and test FASTA sets of mRNA, their CDS, and lncRNA transcripts from curated annotations (e.g., NCBI RefSeq or GENCODE) to train or benchmark lncRNA identification models. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-transcript-dataset-construction))
- **rna-velocity**: --&gt; ([mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-](https://github.com/mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-/tree/HEAD/Skills/Genomics/Single_Cell/rna-velocity) · ★36 · 2 copies)
- **rna-velocity-agent**: --&gt; ([FreedomIntelligence/OpenClaw-Medical-Skills](https://github.com/FreedomIntelligence/OpenClaw-Medical-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/rna-velocity-agent) · ★3.1k · 13 copies)
- **rna-velocity-agent**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/ai-ml/rna-velocity-agent) · ★745)
- **rna-velocity-agent**: AI-powered RNA velocity analysis for predicting cellular state transitions, differentiation trajectories, and dynamic gene regulation from single-cell RNA sequencing data. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/rna-velocity-agent) · ★104 · 3 copies)
- **rna-velocity-agent**: ---name: rna-velocity-agent ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry-data](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry-data/tree/HEAD/ai-ml/rna-velocity-agent) · ★30 · 2 copies)
- **rna-velocity-alignment-score-computation**: Use when you have an AnnData object with spliced/unspliced layers, scvelo RNA velocity, and an ensemble of DTW-aligned simulated trajectories, and need per-cell scores, pseudotime, and cell fate probabilities. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-velocity-alignment-score-computation))
- **rna-velocity-baseline-comparison**: Use when you need to benchmark an RNA-velocity- or manifold-based cell-cycle method (scVelo dynamical, Cyclum, Cyclone, Revelio, DeepCycle) on mESC or human fibroblast h5ad data and quantify agreement of inferred trajectories with annotated phases or DeepCycle's cell_cycle_theta. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-velocity-baseline-comparison))
- **rna-velocity-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when comparing RNA velocity methods on real datasets with known trajectory directions, or when quantitative CBDir/ICCoh benchmark scores are needed to validate velocity estimates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-velocity-benchmark-evaluation))
- **rna-velocity-best-fitted-gene-selection**: Use when a TFvelo run has completed (rc.h5ad output exists) and you need to extract -fitted genes, test KEGG pathway enrichment among them, and verify the sign consistency of top-weighted TFs on specific pathway gene sets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-velocity-best-fitted-gene-selection))
- **rna-velocity-dynamical-model-gene-ranking**: Use when comparing unsupervised feature-selection methods on a differentiation trajectory and you have spliced/unspliced counts so ScVelo's dynamical model can produce a likelihood-ranked gene set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-velocity-dynamical-model-gene-ranking))
- **rna-velocity-estimation**: Use when scRNA-seq data with spliced and unspliced counts (e.g. SMART-Seq2 U2OS cell cycle data) must be converted into velocity vectors and a cell-to-cell transition matrix to support directionality-aware trajectory reconstruction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-velocity-estimation--8ebface7273d0bdb))
- **rna-velocity-estimation**: Use when you have spliced/unspliced count matrices (AnnData/h5ad) from differentiation datasets such as pancreas, dentate gyrus, retina, hindbrain, erythroid or bone marrow, and need directed cell transitions, latent time, or CBDir benchmarking against scVelo. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-velocity-estimation--bf1f63895efaec15))
- **rna-velocity-estimation-spliced-unspliced**: Use when single-cell scRNA-seq data with spliced and unspliced count layers is available and the goal is to visualize predicted transcriptional trajectories as animated arrows/particles in a 3D embedding (e.g., PHATE/UMAP) inside CellexalVR. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-velocity-estimation-spliced-unspliced))
- **rna-velocity-fate-probability-inference**: Use when a pre-processed AnnData with cell-type 'clusters' annotations and a scVelo RNA velocity field is available and you need per-cell fate probabilities or end-fate frequencies toward terminal states (e.g., E15.5 endocrine α, β, ε fates), or to benchmark against CellRank. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-velocity-fate-probability-inference))
- **rna-velocity-inference**: Use when single-cell spliced/unspliced AnnData layers are available and you need velocity fields, latent trajectories, batch-corrected dynamics embeddings, or fate separation across multiple lineages where steady-state RNA velocity assumptions fail. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-velocity-inference))
- **rna-velocity-intron-transcript-extraction**: Use when preparing a kallisto/bustools index for RNA velocity or splicing-aware scRNA-seq quantification, so that pseudoalignment can separate spliced (cDNA) reads from unspliced (intronic) reads. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-velocity-intron-transcript-extraction))
- **rna-velocity-latent-dynamics-modeling**: Use when spliced/unspliced AnnData layers are available and the goal is RNA velocity on multi-lineage, time-dependent, or multi-batch data where strict steady-state assumptions of Velocyto/scVelo fail, or when batch-integrated velocities and latent trajectories are needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-velocity-latent-dynamics-modeling))
- **rna-velocity-latent-time-inference**: Use when multi-batch single-cell transcriptomic data captures a continuous biological process (e.g., erythroid differentiation) and cells must be ordered in pseudo-time to validate that learned latent factors show distinct temporal profiles. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-velocity-latent-time-inference))
- **rna-velocity-moments-preprocessing**: Use when preparing an scRNA-seq AnnData (e.g. adata_mESC.h5ad from Zenodo) that lacks imputed 'Ms'/'Mu' layers, as a mandatory step before running DeepCycle or any unspliced-spliced RNA velocity analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-velocity-moments-preprocessing))
- **rna-velocity-pseudotime-estimation**: Use when scRNA-seq data has pre-computed RNA velocity (scvelo/velocyto), root and terminal cell states are identifiable or supplied, and per-lineage pseudotime must be inferred and validated against a known cell type ordering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-velocity-pseudotime-estimation))
- **rna-velocity-smoothing**: Use when an scRNA-seq AnnData object with spliced counts and scVelo-derived RNA velocity must be denoised into meta-cell-level smoothed velocities as input for trajectory inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-velocity-smoothing))
- **rna-velocity-starter**: Use this starter when a task lands in the `RNA velocity` frontier leaf and the repository has curated resources but no dedicated runtime implementation yet. ([ma-compbio-lab/SkillFoundry](https://github.com/ma-compbio-lab/SkillFoundry/tree/HEAD/skills/transcriptomics/rna-velocity-starter) · ★46)
- **rna-velocity-trajectory**: Infers directional cellular trajectories from scRNA-seq using spliced/unspliced ratios. Use when predicting cell fates, differentiation endpoints, or driver genes from a single snapshot — covers scVelo, VeloVI, Chronocell, and CellRank. Needs intron-aware quantification; for the upstream counting use starsolo-spliced-unspliced. ([tony-zhelonkin/scio](https://github.com/tony-zhelonkin/scio/tree/HEAD/skills/rna-velocity-trajectory) · ★1)
- **rna-velocity-trajectory-inference**: Use when a scRNA-seq AnnData object (with spliced/unspliced counts) needs trajectory inference: recovering parallel branches, within-population sub-flows, and cell pseudotime that direction-free TI methods (e.g., Slingshot) or velocity tools that lack path output (e.g., CellRank) cannot resolve. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-velocity-trajectory-inference))
- **rna-velocity-transition-matrix-construction**: Use when spliced/unspliced AnnData with scvelo-computed RNA velocity is available and a probabilistic cell-state transition matrix is needed to sample differentiation trajectories or estimate root/terminal states. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rna-velocity-transition-matrix-construction))
- **rna-versus-dna-read-classification**: Use when processing sci-L3 paired-end FASTQ reads from the joint RNA/chromatin-accessibility (RNA/ATAC) co-assay, where reads must be labeled RNA or DNA so downstream splitting can separate transcriptome from accessibility data per single cell. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/rna-versus-dna-read-classification))

- **rnacentral-search**: Resolve non-coding RNA identifiers, sequences, types, and cross-references through RNAcentral.
